#include "app_nettools.h"
#include "ui_common.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "ping/ping_sock.h"
#include <arpa/inet.h>
#include "lwip/sockets.h"
#include "lwip/netdb.h"
#include "bsp/esp-bsp.h"

static const char *TAG = "NetTools";

// Elementos Visuais Globais do App
static lv_obj_t *scr_menu_nettools = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *list_tools = NULL;

// ---------------------------------------------------------
// COMPONENTES - PING UTILITY
// ---------------------------------------------------------
static lv_obj_t *scr_ping = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *ta_ip = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *kb_ping = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *btn_start_ping = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *label_ping_output = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *container_output = NULL;
static esp_ping_handle_t ping_handle = NULL;

#define MAX_MSG_LEN 128
static char ping_msg_queue[MAX_MSG_LEN] = {0};
static volatile bool has_new_msg = false;

// ---------------------------------------------------------
// COMPONENTES - PORT DISCOVERY
// ---------------------------------------------------------
static lv_obj_t *scr_port = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *ta_scan_ip = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *ta_scan_port = NULL; // Nova caixa para porta opcional
static lv_obj_t *kb_scan = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *btn_start_scan = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *label_scan_output = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *container_scan_output = NULL;

static TaskHandle_t scan_task_handle = NULL;
static volatile bool stop_scan_task = false;
static char scan_msg_queue[MAX_MSG_LEN] = {0};
static volatile bool has_scan_msg = false;

// VariÃ¡veis para transportar os dados do LVGL para a Task
static char target_scan_ip[32];
static uint16_t target_scan_port = 0;

// Top 22 Portas (PadrÃµes + Homelab)
const uint16_t top_ports[] = {
    21, 22, 23, 53, 80, 111, 139, 443, 445, // Web e Infra bÃ¡sica
    1883,  // MQTT
    3306,  // MySQL / MariaDB
    3389,  // RDP (Windows Remote Desktop)
    7125,  // Klipper / Moonraker (ImpressÃ£o 3D)
    8000,  // Portainer Edge / Proxies
    8006,  // Proxmox VE
    8080,  // Web-Alt
    8123,  // Home Assistant
    8443,  // Web-Alt HTTPS / Unifi Controller
    9000,  // Portainer GUI
    9443,  // Portainer HTTPS
    32400, // Plex Media Server
    61208  // Glances (Monitoramento)
};

// =========================================================
// EVENTOS DE GESTOS E TOQUE
// =========================================================
static void local_screen_pressed_cb(lv_event_t * e) {
    if(lv_indev_active()) {
        lv_point_t p;
        lv_indev_get_point(lv_indev_active(), &p);
        touch_start_x = p.x; 
    }
    reset_inactivity_timer();
}

static void tools_gesture_cb(lv_event_t * e) {
    lv_dir_t dir = lv_indev_get_gesture_dir(lv_indev_active());
    if(dir == LV_DIR_RIGHT && touch_start_x < 20) {
        lv_scr_load_anim(scr_apps, LV_SCR_LOAD_ANIM_MOVE_RIGHT, 300, 0, false);
    }
}

static void sub_screen_ping_gesture_cb(lv_event_t * e) {
    lv_dir_t dir = lv_indev_get_gesture_dir(lv_indev_active());
    if(dir == LV_DIR_RIGHT && touch_start_x < 20) {
        if (ping_handle) {
            esp_ping_stop(ping_handle);
            esp_ping_delete_session(ping_handle);
            ping_handle = NULL;
        }
        lv_scr_load_anim(scr_menu_nettools, LV_SCR_LOAD_ANIM_MOVE_RIGHT, 300, 0, false);
    }
}

static void sub_screen_scan_gesture_cb(lv_event_t * e) {
    lv_dir_t dir = lv_indev_get_gesture_dir(lv_indev_active());
    if(dir == LV_DIR_RIGHT && touch_start_x < 20) {
        // Sinaliza para a Task do Scanner parar de procurar e morrer
        if (scan_task_handle != NULL) {
            stop_scan_task = true; 
        }
        lv_scr_load_anim(scr_menu_nettools, LV_SCR_LOAD_ANIM_MOVE_RIGHT, 300, 0, false);
    }
}

// =========================================================
// ATUALIZADOR SEGURO DE UI (CORE 0)
// =========================================================
static void ui_update_timer_cb(lv_timer_t * timer) {
    // Atualiza mensagens do Ping
    if (has_new_msg) {
        if (bsp_display_lock(pdMS_TO_TICKS(50))) {
            if (label_ping_output && container_output) {
                lv_label_ins_text(label_ping_output, LV_LABEL_POS_LAST, ping_msg_queue);
                lv_obj_scroll_to_y(container_output, LV_COORD_MAX, LV_ANIM_ON);
            }
            bsp_display_unlock();
        }
        has_new_msg = false;
    }

    // Atualiza mensagens do Port Scanner
    if (has_scan_msg) {
        if (bsp_display_lock(pdMS_TO_TICKS(50))) {
            if (label_scan_output && container_scan_output) {
                lv_label_ins_text(label_scan_output, LV_LABEL_POS_LAST, scan_msg_queue);
                lv_obj_scroll_to_y(container_scan_output, LV_COORD_MAX, LV_ANIM_ON);
            }
            bsp_display_unlock();
        }
        has_scan_msg = false;
    }
}

// =========================================================
// LÃ“GICA DO PING
// =========================================================
static void cmd_ping_on_ping_success(esp_ping_handle_t h, void *args) {
    if (has_new_msg) return; 

    uint8_t ttl;
    uint16_t seqno;
    uint32_t elapsed_time, recv_len;
    ip_addr_t target_addr;
    
    esp_ping_get_profile(h, ESP_PING_PROF_TTL, &ttl, sizeof(ttl));
    esp_ping_get_profile(h, ESP_PING_PROF_SEQNO, &seqno, sizeof(seqno));
    esp_ping_get_profile(h, ESP_PING_PROF_SIZE, &recv_len, sizeof(recv_len));
    esp_ping_get_profile(h, ESP_PING_PROF_TIMEGAP, &elapsed_time, sizeof(elapsed_time));
    esp_ping_get_profile(h, ESP_PING_PROF_IPADDR, &target_addr, sizeof(target_addr));

    snprintf(ping_msg_queue, sizeof(ping_msg_queue), "\n%lu bytes de %s: seq=%d ttl=%d tempo=%lu ms",
             (unsigned long)recv_len, ipaddr_ntoa(&target_addr), seqno, ttl, (unsigned long)elapsed_time);
    has_new_msg = true;
}

static void cmd_ping_on_ping_timeout(esp_ping_handle_t h, void *args) {
    if (has_new_msg) return;
    snprintf(ping_msg_queue, sizeof(ping_msg_queue), "\nSolicitacao expirada (Timeout).");
    has_new_msg = true;
}

static void cmd_ping_on_ping_end(esp_ping_handle_t h, void *args) {
    if (has_new_msg) return;
    snprintf(ping_msg_queue, sizeof(ping_msg_queue), "\n--- Teste Concluido ---");
    has_new_msg = true;
    ping_handle = NULL;
}

static void hide_ping_keyboard(void * obj) {
    if (bsp_display_lock(0)) {
        lv_obj_add_flag(kb_ping, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);
        lv_obj_remove_flag(container_output, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);
        bsp_display_unlock();
    }
}

static void start_ping_action(void) {
    const char *target_ip = lv_textarea_get_text(ta_ip);
    if (strlen(target_ip) == 0) return;

    lv_async_call(hide_ping_keyboard, NULL);

    if (bsp_display_lock(0)) {
        lv_label_set_text_fmt(label_ping_output, "Disparando contra %s:", target_ip);
        bsp_display_unlock();
    }

    ip_addr_t target_addr;
    memset(&target_addr, 0, sizeof(target_addr));
    if (ipaddr_aton(target_ip, &target_addr) == 0) {
        if (bsp_display_lock(0)) {
            lv_label_set_text(label_ping_output, "Erro: Endereco IP Invalido.");
            bsp_display_unlock();
        }
        return;
    }

    esp_ping_config_t ping_config = ESP_PING_DEFAULT_CONFIG();
    ping_config.target_addr = target_addr;
    ping_config.count = 5; 
    ping_config.interval_ms = 1000; 

    esp_ping_callbacks_t cbs;
    memset(&cbs, 0, sizeof(cbs));
    cbs.on_ping_success = cmd_ping_on_ping_success;
    cbs.on_ping_timeout = cmd_ping_on_ping_timeout;
    cbs.on_ping_end = cmd_ping_on_ping_end;

    if (ping_handle) {
        esp_ping_stop(ping_handle);
        esp_ping_delete_session(ping_handle);
    }
    esp_ping_new_session(&ping_config, &cbs, &ping_handle);
    esp_ping_start(ping_handle);
}

// =========================================================
// LÃ“GICA DO PORT DISCOVERY (FREERTOS TASK)
// =========================================================

// FunÃ§Ã£o helper para enviar mensagens da Task para o LVGL
static void send_scan_msg(const char* msg) {
    while (has_scan_msg && !stop_scan_task) {
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(10)); // Aguarda o LVGL consumir a msg anterior
    }
    if (!stop_scan_task) {
        strncpy(scan_msg_queue, msg, MAX_MSG_LEN - 1);
        has_scan_msg = true;
    }
}

// Tenta abrir Socket TCP com Timeout rÃ¡pido
static bool is_port_open(const char *ip, uint16_t port) {
    int sock = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    if (sock < 0) return false;

    // Configura Socket como NÃ£o-Bloqueante
    int flags = fcntl(sock, F_GETFL, 0);
    fcntl(sock, F_SETFL, flags | O_NONBLOCK);

    struct sockaddr_in server_addr;
    memset(&server_addr, 0, sizeof(server_addr));
    server_addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    server_addr.sin_port = htons(port);
    inet_pton(AF_INET, ip, &server_addr.sin_addr);

    // Inicia a tentativa de conexÃ£o
    int res = connect(sock, (struct sockaddr *)&server_addr, sizeof(server_addr));
    if (res < 0 && errno != EINPROGRESS && errno != EWOULDBLOCK) {
        close(sock);
        return false;
    }

    // Configura o Timeout do Select (300 milissegundos)
    fd_set fdset;
    FD_ZERO(&fdset);
    FD_SET(sock, &fdset);
    struct timeval tv;
    tv.tv_sec = 0;
    tv.tv_usec = 300000; 

    res = select(sock + 1, NULL, &fdset, NULL, &tv);
    bool is_open = false;
    if (res > 0) { // O Socket respondeu
        int so_error = 0;
        socklen_t len = sizeof(so_error);
        getsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_ERROR, &so_error, &len);
        if (so_error == 0) {
            is_open = true; // Porta aberta!
        }
    }
    
    close(sock);
    return is_open;
}

// A Task que roda em background
static void port_scan_task(void *pvParameters) {
    send_scan_msg("\nIniciando varredura...");
    vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(100)); // Pequena pausa para a UI atualizar

    if (target_scan_port > 0) {
        // Varredura de Porta Ãšnica Customizada
        char buf[64];
        snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "\nTestando porta %d...", target_scan_port);
        send_scan_msg(buf);
        
        if (is_port_open(target_scan_ip, target_scan_port)) {
            snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "\n[+] Porta %d ABERTA", target_scan_port);
            send_scan_msg(buf);
        } else {
            snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "\n[-] Porta %d fechada", target_scan_port);
            send_scan_msg(buf);
        }
    } else {
        // Varredura do Homelab Top 22
        int total_ports = sizeof(top_ports) / sizeof(top_ports[0]);
        for (int i = 0; i < total_ports; i++) {
            if (stop_scan_task) break; // Interrompe se o usuÃ¡rio arrastar para sair da tela
            
            if (is_port_open(target_scan_ip, top_ports[i])) {
                char buf[64];
                snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "\n[+] Porta %d ABERTA", top_ports[i]);
                send_scan_msg(buf);
            }
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(50)); // Respiro pro chip de rede
        }
    }
    
    if (!stop_scan_task) {
        send_scan_msg("\n--- Teste Concluido ---");
    }
    
    scan_task_handle = NULL;
    vTaskDelete(NULL); // Mata a task
}

static void hide_scan_keyboard(void * obj) {
    if (bsp_display_lock(0)) {
        lv_obj_add_flag(kb_scan, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);
        lv_obj_remove_flag(container_scan_output, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);
        bsp_display_unlock();
    }
}

static void start_scan_action(void) {
    const char *ip_str = lv_textarea_get_text(ta_scan_ip);
    const char *port_str = lv_textarea_get_text(ta_scan_port);
    
    if (strlen(ip_str) == 0) return;

    // Salva nas globais para a Task ler
    strncpy(target_scan_ip, ip_str, sizeof(target_scan_ip) - 1);
    target_scan_port = (strlen(port_str) > 0) ? atoi(port_str) : 0;

    lv_async_call(hide_scan_keyboard, NULL);

    if (bsp_display_lock(0)) {
        if (target_scan_port > 0) {
            lv_label_set_text_fmt(label_scan_output, "Alvo: %s:%d", target_scan_ip, target_scan_port);
        } else {
            lv_label_set_text_fmt(label_scan_output, "Scan Top 20: %s", target_scan_ip);
        }
        bsp_display_unlock();
    }

    // Se jÃ¡ tinha uma task rodando, nÃ£o sobrepÃµe
    if (scan_task_handle == NULL) {
        stop_scan_task = false;
        has_scan_msg = false;
        // Cria a Task na CPU 1 com stack generoso de 4KB
        xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(port_scan_task, "port_scan", 4096, NULL, 5, &scan_task_handle, 1);
    }
}

// =========================================================
// INICIALIZAÃ‡ÃƒO DA INTERFACE (MUITAS TELAS)
// =========================================================
void app_nettools_init(void) {
    lv_timer_create(ui_update_timer_cb, 100, NULL); // Timer Ãºnico que cuida de tudo

    // 1. TELA MENU PRINCIPAL
    scr_menu_nettools = lv_obj_create(NULL);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(scr_menu_nettools, lv_color_black(), 0);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(scr_menu_nettools, local_screen_pressed_cb, LV_EVENT_PRESSED, NULL);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(scr_menu_nettools, tools_gesture_cb, LV_EVENT_GESTURE, NULL);

    lv_obj_t * title = lv_label_create(scr_menu_nettools);
    lv_label_set_text(title, "Ferramentas de Rede");
    lv_obj_set_style_text_font(title, &lv_font_montserrat_20, 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(title, lv_color_white(), 0);
    lv_obj_align(title, LV_ALIGN_TOP_LEFT, 50, 30);

    list_tools = lv_list_create(scr_menu_nettools);
    lv_obj_set_size(list_tools, 310, 350);
    lv_obj_align(list_tools, LV_ALIGN_BOTTOM_MID, 0, -30);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(list_tools, lv_color_black(), 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_border_width(list_tools, 0, 0);

    // BotÃ£o Ping
    lv_obj_t * btn_ping = lv_list_add_button(list_tools, LV_SYMBOL_LOOP, "Ping Utility");
    style_list_item(btn_ping); 
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(btn_ping, [](lv_event_t *e){
        lv_scr_load_anim(scr_ping, LV_SCR_LOAD_ANIM_MOVE_LEFT, 300, 0, false);
    }, LV_EVENT_CLICKED, NULL);

    // BotÃ£o Port Discovery
    lv_obj_t * btn_port = lv_list_add_button(list_tools, LV_SYMBOL_EYE_OPEN, "Port Discovery");
    style_list_item(btn_port);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(btn_port, [](lv_event_t *e){
        lv_scr_load_anim(scr_port, LV_SCR_LOAD_ANIM_MOVE_LEFT, 300, 0, false);
    }, LV_EVENT_CLICKED, NULL);


    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. TELA INTERNA: PING
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    scr_ping = lv_obj_create(NULL);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(scr_ping, lv_color_black(), 0);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(scr_ping, local_screen_pressed_cb, LV_EVENT_PRESSED, NULL);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(scr_ping, sub_screen_ping_gesture_cb, LV_EVENT_GESTURE, NULL);

    lv_obj_t * ping_title = lv_label_create(scr_ping);
    lv_label_set_text(ping_title, "ICMP Ping");
    lv_obj_set_style_text_font(ping_title, &lv_font_montserrat_20, 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(ping_title, lv_color_white(), 0);
    lv_obj_align(ping_title, LV_ALIGN_TOP_LEFT, 50, 20);

    ta_ip = lv_textarea_create(scr_ping);
    lv_textarea_set_one_line(ta_ip, true);
    lv_textarea_set_placeholder_text(ta_ip, "192.168.1.1");
    lv_obj_set_width(ta_ip, 220);
    lv_obj_align(ta_ip, LV_ALIGN_TOP_LEFT, 30, 60);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(ta_ip, local_screen_pressed_cb, LV_EVENT_PRESSED, NULL);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(ta_ip, [](lv_event_t *e){
        lv_obj_remove_flag(kb_ping, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);
        lv_obj_add_flag(container_output, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN); 
    }, LV_EVENT_CLICKED, NULL);

    btn_start_ping = lv_button_create(scr_ping);
    lv_obj_set_size(btn_start_ping, 90, 45);
    lv_obj_align(btn_start_ping, LV_ALIGN_TOP_RIGHT, -30, 60);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(btn_start_ping, lv_color_hex(0x28A745), 0); 
    lv_obj_t * btn_lbl = lv_label_create(btn_start_ping);
    lv_label_set_text(btn_lbl, "OK");
    lv_obj_center(btn_lbl);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(btn_start_ping, [](lv_event_t *e) { start_ping_action(); }, LV_EVENT_CLICKED, NULL);

    container_output = lv_obj_create(scr_ping);
    lv_obj_set_size(container_output, 350, 340);
    lv_obj_align(container_output, LV_ALIGN_BOTTOM_MID, 0, -20);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(container_output, lv_color_hex(0x111111), 0); 
    lv_obj_set_style_border_width(container_output, 0, 0);

    label_ping_output = lv_label_create(container_output);
    lv_obj_set_width(label_ping_output, 320);
    lv_label_set_text(label_ping_output, "Pronto para disparo.");
    lv_obj_set_style_text_font(label_ping_output, &lv_font_montserrat_14, 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(label_ping_output, lv_color_hex(0x00FF00), 0); 

    kb_ping = lv_keyboard_create(scr_ping);
    lv_keyboard_set_textarea(kb_ping, ta_ip);
    lv_keyboard_set_mode(kb_ping, LV_KEYBOARD_MODE_NUMBER); 
    lv_obj_add_flag(kb_ping, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(kb_ping, [](lv_event_t *e) { start_ping_action(); }, LV_EVENT_READY, NULL);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(kb_ping, [](lv_event_t *e) { lv_async_call(hide_ping_keyboard, NULL); }, LV_EVENT_CANCEL, NULL);


    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. TELA INTERNA: PORT DISCOVERY
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    scr_port = lv_obj_create(NULL);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(scr_port, lv_color_black(), 0);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(scr_port, local_screen_pressed_cb, LV_EVENT_PRESSED, NULL);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(scr_port, sub_screen_scan_gesture_cb, LV_EVENT_GESTURE, NULL);

    lv_obj_t * scan_title = lv_label_create(scr_port);
    lv_label_set_text(scan_title, "Port Scanner");
    lv_obj_set_style_text_font(scan_title, &lv_font_montserrat_20, 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(scan_title, lv_color_white(), 0);
    lv_obj_align(scan_title, LV_ALIGN_TOP_LEFT, 50, 20);

    // Caixa de IP
    ta_scan_ip = lv_textarea_create(scr_port);
    lv_textarea_set_one_line(ta_scan_ip, true);
    lv_textarea_set_placeholder_text(ta_scan_ip, "IP Alvo");
    lv_obj_set_width(ta_scan_ip, 160);
    lv_obj_align(ta_scan_ip, LV_ALIGN_TOP_LEFT, 30, 60);
    
    // Caixa de Porta
    ta_scan_port = lv_textarea_create(scr_port);
    lv_textarea_set_one_line(ta_scan_port, true);
    lv_textarea_set_placeholder_text(ta_scan_port, "Porta");
    lv_obj_set_width(ta_scan_port, 80);
    lv_obj_align(ta_scan_port, LV_ALIGN_TOP_LEFT, 200, 60);

    // BotÃ£o Scan
    btn_start_scan = lv_button_create(scr_port);
    lv_obj_set_size(btn_start_scan, 75, 45);
    lv_obj_align(btn_start_scan, LV_ALIGN_TOP_RIGHT, -30, 60);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(btn_start_scan, lv_color_hex(0x007BFF), 0); // Azul
    lv_obj_t * btn_scan_lbl = lv_label_create(btn_start_scan);
    lv_label_set_text(btn_scan_lbl, "SCAN");
    lv_obj_center(btn_scan_lbl);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(btn_start_scan, [](lv_event_t *e) { start_scan_action(); }, LV_EVENT_CLICKED, NULL);

    // Terminal do Scanner
    container_scan_output = lv_obj_create(scr_port);
    lv_obj_set_size(container_scan_output, 350, 340);
    lv_obj_align(container_scan_output, LV_ALIGN_BOTTOM_MID, 0, -20);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(container_scan_output, lv_color_hex(0x111111), 0); 
    lv_obj_set_style_border_width(container_scan_output, 0, 0);

    label_scan_output = lv_label_create(container_scan_output);
    lv_obj_set_width(label_scan_output, 320);
    lv_label_set_text(label_scan_output, "P/ vazio: Top22 Homelab\nP/ num: Alvo Unico.");
    lv_obj_set_style_text_font(label_scan_output, &lv_font_montserrat_14, 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(label_scan_output, lv_color_hex(0x00FF00), 0); 

    // Teclado compartilhado
    kb_scan = lv_keyboard_create(scr_port);
    lv_keyboard_set_mode(kb_scan, LV_KEYBOARD_MODE_NUMBER); 
    lv_obj_add_flag(kb_scan, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);

    // Alterna o foco do teclado dinamicamente
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(ta_scan_ip, [](lv_event_t *e){
        lv_keyboard_set_textarea(kb_scan, ta_scan_ip);
        lv_obj_remove_flag(kb_scan, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);
        lv_obj_add_flag(container_scan_output, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN); 
    }, LV_EVENT_CLICKED, NULL);

    lv_obj_add_event_cb(ta_scan_port, [](lv_event_t *e){
        lv_keyboard_set_textarea(kb_scan, ta_scan_port);
        lv_obj_remove_flag(kb_scan, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);
        lv_obj_add_flag(container_scan_output, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN); 
    }, LV_EVENT_CLICKED, NULL);
    
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(kb_scan, [](lv_event_t *e) { start_scan_action(); }, LV_EVENT_READY, NULL);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(kb_scan, [](lv_event_t *e) { lv_async_call(hide_scan_keyboard, NULL); }, LV_EVENT_CANCEL, NULL);
}

lv_obj_t * app_nettools_get_screen(void) { return scr_menu_nettools; }

#include "app_sysinfo.h"
#include "esp_system.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include "ui_common.h"

static lv_obj_t *scr_app_sysinfo = NULL;
static lv_obj_t *label_sysinfo = NULL;
static lv_timer_t *sysinfo_timer = NULL; 


// [NOVO] FunÃ§Ã£o local do componente
static void local_screen_pressed_cb(lv_event_t * e) {
    lv_indev_t * indev = lv_indev_active();
    if(indev) {
        lv_point_t p;
        lv_indev_get_point(indev, &p);
        touch_start_x = p.x; 
    }
    // [NOVO] Reseta o timer global de energia ao tocar na tela do app
    reset_inactivity_timer();
}

static void sysinfo_gesture_cb(lv_event_t * e) {
    lv_indev_t * indev = lv_indev_active();
    if(indev == NULL) return;
    lv_dir_t dir = lv_indev_get_gesture_dir(indev);
    if(dir == LV_DIR_RIGHT && touch_start_x < 20) {
        lv_scr_load_anim(scr_apps, LV_SCR_LOAD_ANIM_MOVE_RIGHT, 300, 0, false);
    }
}

static void sysinfo_timer_cb(lv_timer_t * timer) {
    if (lv_screen_active() == scr_app_sysinfo) {
        app_sysinfo_update();
    }
}

void app_sysinfo_init(void) {
    scr_app_sysinfo = lv_obj_create(NULL);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(scr_app_sysinfo, lv_color_black(), 0);
    
    // Usando a funÃ§Ã£o local
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(scr_app_sysinfo, local_screen_pressed_cb, LV_EVENT_PRESSED, NULL); 
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(scr_app_sysinfo, sysinfo_gesture_cb, LV_EVENT_GESTURE, NULL);

    lv_obj_t * title = lv_label_create(scr_app_sysinfo);
    lv_label_set_text(title, "Sistema");
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(title, lv_color_white(), 0); 
    lv_obj_set_style_text_font(title, &lv_font_montserrat_20, 0);
    lv_obj_align(title, LV_ALIGN_TOP_LEFT, 50, 30);

    label_sysinfo = lv_label_create(scr_app_sysinfo);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(label_sysinfo, lv_color_white(), 0); 
    lv_obj_set_style_text_font(label_sysinfo, &lv_font_montserrat_20, 0);
    lv_obj_align(label_sysinfo, LV_ALIGN_TOP_LEFT, 40, 80);

    sysinfo_timer = lv_timer_create(sysinfo_timer_cb, 1000, NULL);
}

void app_sysinfo_update(void) {
    if (!label_sysinfo) return;
    
    char buf[128];
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), 
             "RAM Livre: %lu KB\n"
             "Min. Heap: %lu KB\n"
             "Chip: ESP32-S3\n"
             "Freq: 160 MHz", 
             esp_get_free_heap_size() / 1024,
             esp_get_minimum_free_heap_size() / 1024);
    
    lv_label_set_text(label_sysinfo, buf);
}

lv_obj_t * app_sysinfo_get_screen(void) { return scr_app_sysinfo; }

#pragma once

#include <sys/cdefs.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include "esp_codec_dev.h"
#include "esp_err.h"
#include "driver/gpio.h"
#include "driver/i2s_std.h"
#include "audio_player.h"
#include "file_iterator.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define CODEC_DEFAULT_SAMPLE_RATE           (16000)
#define CODEC_DEFAULT_BIT_WIDTH             (16)
#define CODEC_DEFAULT_ADC_VOLUME            (24.0)
#define CODEC_DEFAULT_CHANNEL               (2)
#define CODEC_DEFAULT_VOLUME                (60)

#define BSP_LCD_BACKLIGHT_BRIGHTNESS_MAX    (95)
#define BSP_LCD_BACKLIGHT_BRIGHTNESS_MIN    (0)
#define LCD_LEDC_CH                         (CONFIG_BSP_DISPLAY_BRIGHTNESS_LEDC_CH)

/**************************************************************************************************
 * BSP Extra interface
 * Mainly provided some I2S Codec interfaces.
 **************************************************************************************************/
/**
 * @brief Player set mute.
 *
 * @param enable: true or false
 *
 * @return
 *    - ESP_OK: Success
 *    - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_mute_set(bool enable);

/**
 * @brief Player set volume.
 *
 * @param volume: volume set
 * @param volume_set: volume set response
 *
 * @return
 *    - ESP_OK: Success
 *    - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_volume_set(int volume, int *volume_set);

/** 
 * @brief Player get volume.
 * 
 * @return
 *   - volume: volume get
 */
int bsp_extra_codec_volume_get(void);

/**
 * @brief Stop I2S function.
 *
 * @return
 *    - ESP_OK: Success
 *    - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_dev_stop(void);

/**
 * @brief Resume I2S function.
 *
 * @return
 *    - ESP_OK: Success
 *    - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_dev_resume(void);

/**
 * @brief Set I2S format to codec.
 *
 * @param rate: Sample rate of sample
 * @param bits_cfg: Bit lengths of one channel data
 * @param ch: Channels of sample
 *
 * @return
 *    - ESP_OK: Success
 *    - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_set_fs(uint32_t rate, uint32_t bits_cfg, i2s_slot_mode_t ch);

/**
 * @brief Read data from recoder.
 *
 * @param audio_buffer: The pointer of receiving data buffer
 * @param len: Max data buffer length
 * @param bytes_read: Byte number that actually be read, can be NULL if not needed
 * @param timeout_ms: Max block time
 *
 * @return
 *    - ESP_OK: Success
 *    - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_i2s_read(void *audio_buffer, size_t len, size_t *bytes_read, uint32_t timeout_ms);

/**
 * @brief Write data to player.
 *
 * @param audio_buffer: The pointer of sent data buffer
 * @param len: Max data buffer length
 * @param bytes_written: Byte number that actually be sent, can be NULL if not needed
 * @param timeout_ms: Max block time
 *
 * @return
 *    - ESP_OK: Success
 *    - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_i2s_write(void *audio_buffer, size_t len, size_t *bytes_written, uint32_t timeout_ms);


/**
 * @brief Initialize codec play and record handle.
 *
 * @return
 *      - ESP_OK: Success
 *      - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_init();

/**
 * @brief Initialize audio player task.
 *
 * @param path file path
 *
 * @return
 *      - ESP_OK: Success
 *      - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_player_init(void);

/**
 * @brief Delete audio player task.
 *
 * @return
 *      - ESP_OK: Success
 *      - Others: Fail
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_player_del(void);

/**
 * @brief Initialize a file iterator instance
 *
 * @param path The file path for the iterator.
 * @param ret_instance A pointer to the file iterator instance to be returned.
 * @return
 *     - ESP_OK: Successfully initialized the file iterator instance.
 *     - ESP_FAIL: Failed to initialize the file iterator instance due to invalid parameters or memory allocation failure.
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_file_instance_init(const char *path, file_iterator_instance_t **ret_instance);

/**
 * @brief Play the audio file at the specified index in the file iterator
 *
 * @param instance The file iterator instance.
 * @param index The index of the file to play within the iterator.
 * @return
 *     - ESP_OK: Successfully started playing the audio file.
 *     - ESP_FAIL: Failed to play the audio file due to invalid parameters or file access issues.
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_player_play_index(file_iterator_instance_t *instance, int index);

/**
 * @brief Play the audio file specified by the file path
 *
 * @param file_path The path to the audio file to be played.
 * @return
 *     - ESP_OK: Successfully started playing the audio file.
 *     - ESP_FAIL: Failed to play the audio file due to file access issues.
 */
esp_err_t bsp_extra_player_play_file(const char *file_path);

/**
 * @brief Register a callback function for the audio player
 *
 * @param cb The callback function to be registered.
 * @param user_data User data to be passed to the callback function.
 */
void bsp_extra_player_register_callback(audio_player_cb_t cb, void *user_data);

/**
 * @brief Check if the specified audio file is currently playing
 *
 * @param file_path The path to the audio file to check.
 * @return
 *     - true: The specified audio file is currently playing.
 *     - false: The specified audio file is not currently playing.
 */
bool bsp_extra_player_is_playing_by_path(const char *file_path);

/**
 * @brief Check if the audio file at the specified index is currently playing
 *
 * @param instance The file iterator instance.
 * @param index The index of the file to check.
 * @return
 *     - true: The audio file at the specified index is currently playing.
 *     - false: The audio file at the specified index is not currently playing.
 */
bool bsp_extra_player_is_playing_by_index(file_iterator_instance_t *instance, int index);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


=== ARQUIVO: components/ ===

/*
 * SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2015-2024 Espressif Systems (Shanghai) CO LTD
 *
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <string.h>
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "esp_check.h"
#include "esp_codec_dev_defaults.h"
#include "esp_err.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "esp_vfs_fat.h"
#include "driver/i2c.h"
#include "driver/i2s_std.h"
#include "driver/gpio.h"
#include "driver/ledc.h"

#include "bsp/esp-bsp.h"
#include "bsp_board_extra.h"

static const char *TAG = "bsp_extra_board";

static esp_codec_dev_handle_t play_dev_handle;
static esp_codec_dev_handle_t record_dev_handle;

static bool _is_audio_init = false;
static bool _is_player_init = false;
static int _vloume_intensity = CODEC_DEFAULT_VOLUME;

static audio_player_cb_t audio_idle_callback = NULL;
static void *audio_idle_cb_user_data = NULL;
static char audio_file_path[128];

/**************************************************************************************************
 *
 * Extra Board Function
 *
 **************************************************************************************************/

static esp_err_t audio_mute_function(AUDIO_PLAYER_MUTE_SETTING setting)
{
    // Volume saved when muting and restored when unmuting. Restoring volume is necessary
    // as es8311_set_voice_mute(true) results in voice volume (REG32) being set to zero.

    bsp_extra_codec_mute_set(setting == AUDIO_PLAYER_MUTE ? true : false);

    // restore the voice volume upon unmuting
    if (setting == AUDIO_PLAYER_UNMUTE) {
        ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(esp_codec_dev_set_out_vol(play_dev_handle, _vloume_intensity), TAG, "Set Codec volume failed");
    }

    return ESP_OK;
}

static void audio_callback(audio_player_cb_ctx_t *ctx)
{
    if (audio_idle_callback) {
        ctx->user_ctx = audio_idle_cb_user_data;
        audio_idle_callback(ctx);
    }
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_i2s_read(void *audio_buffer, size_t len, size_t *bytes_read, uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    esp_err_t ret = ESP_OK;
    ret = esp_codec_dev_read(record_dev_handle, audio_buffer, len);
    *bytes_read = len;
    return ret;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_i2s_write(void *audio_buffer, size_t len, size_t *bytes_written, uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    esp_err_t ret = ESP_OK;
    ret = esp_codec_dev_write(play_dev_handle, audio_buffer, len);
    *bytes_written = len;
    return ret;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_set_fs(uint32_t rate, uint32_t bits_cfg, i2s_slot_mode_t ch)
{
    esp_err_t ret = ESP_OK;

    esp_codec_dev_sample_info_t fs = {
        .sample_rate = rate,
        .channel = ch,
        .bits_per_sample = bits_cfg,
    };

    if (play_dev_handle) {
        ret = esp_codec_dev_close(play_dev_handle);
    }
    if (record_dev_handle) {
        ret |= esp_codec_dev_close(record_dev_handle);
        ret |= esp_codec_dev_set_in_gain(record_dev_handle, CODEC_DEFAULT_ADC_VOLUME);
    }

    if (play_dev_handle) {
        ret |= esp_codec_dev_open(play_dev_handle, &fs);
    }
    if (record_dev_handle) {
        ret |= esp_codec_dev_open(record_dev_handle, &fs);
    }
    return ret;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_volume_set(int volume, int *volume_set)
{
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(esp_codec_dev_set_out_vol(play_dev_handle, volume), TAG, "Set Codec volume failed");
    _vloume_intensity = volume;

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Setting volume: %d", volume);

    return ESP_OK;
}

int bsp_extra_codec_volume_get(void)
{
    return _vloume_intensity;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_mute_set(bool enable)
{
    esp_err_t ret = ESP_OK;
    ret = esp_codec_dev_set_out_mute(play_dev_handle, enable);
    return ret;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_dev_stop(void)
{
    esp_err_t ret = ESP_OK;

    if (play_dev_handle) {
        ret = esp_codec_dev_close(play_dev_handle);
    }

    if (record_dev_handle) {
        ret = esp_codec_dev_close(record_dev_handle);
    }
    return ret;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_dev_resume(void)
{
    return bsp_extra_codec_set_fs(CODEC_DEFAULT_SAMPLE_RATE, CODEC_DEFAULT_BIT_WIDTH, CODEC_DEFAULT_CHANNEL);
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_codec_init()
{
    if (_is_audio_init) {
        return ESP_OK;
    }

    play_dev_handle = bsp_audio_codec_speaker_init();
    assert((play_dev_handle) && "play_dev_handle not initialized");

    record_dev_handle = bsp_audio_codec_microphone_init();
    assert((record_dev_handle) && "record_dev_handle not initialized");

    bsp_extra_codec_set_fs(CODEC_DEFAULT_SAMPLE_RATE, CODEC_DEFAULT_BIT_WIDTH, CODEC_DEFAULT_CHANNEL);

    _is_audio_init = true;

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_player_init(void)
{
    if (_is_player_init) {
        return ESP_OK;
    }

    audio_player_config_t config = { .mute_fn = audio_mute_function,
                                     .write_fn = bsp_extra_i2s_write,
                                     .clk_set_fn = bsp_extra_codec_set_fs,
                                     .priority = 5
                                   };
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(audio_player_new(config), TAG, "audio_player_init failed");
    audio_player_callback_register(audio_callback, NULL);

    _is_player_init = true;

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_player_del(void)
{
    _is_player_init = false;

    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(audio_player_delete(), TAG, "audio_player_delete failed");

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_file_instance_init(const char *path, file_iterator_instance_t **ret_instance)
{
    ESP_RETURN_ON_FALSE(path, ESP_FAIL, TAG, "path is NULL");
    ESP_RETURN_ON_FALSE(ret_instance, ESP_FAIL, TAG, "ret_instance is NULL");

    file_iterator_instance_t *file_iterator = file_iterator_new(path);
    ESP_RETURN_ON_FALSE(file_iterator, ESP_FAIL, TAG, "file_iterator_new failed, %s", path);

    *ret_instance = file_iterator;

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_player_play_index(file_iterator_instance_t *instance, int index)
{
    ESP_RETURN_ON_FALSE(instance, ESP_FAIL, TAG, "instance is NULL");

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "play_index(%d)", index);
    char filename[128];
    int retval = file_iterator_get_full_path_from_index(instance, index, filename, sizeof(filename));
    ESP_RETURN_ON_FALSE(retval != 0, ESP_FAIL, TAG, "file_iterator_get_full_path_from_index failed");

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "opening file '%s'", filename);
    FILE *fp = fopen(filename, "rb");
    ESP_RETURN_ON_FALSE(fp, ESP_FAIL, TAG, "unable to open file");

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Playing '%s'", filename);
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(audio_player_play(fp), TAG, "audio_player_play failed");

    memcpy(audio_file_path, filename, sizeof(audio_file_path));

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t bsp_extra_player_play_file(const char *file_path)
{
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "opening file '%s'", file_path);
    FILE *fp = fopen(file_path, "rb");
    ESP_RETURN_ON_FALSE(fp, ESP_FAIL, TAG, "unable to open file");

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Playing '%s'", file_path);
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(audio_player_play(fp), TAG, "audio_player_play failed");

    memcpy(audio_file_path, file_path, sizeof(audio_file_path));

    return ESP_OK;
}

void bsp_extra_player_register_callback(audio_player_cb_t cb, void *user_data)
{
    audio_idle_callback = cb;
    audio_idle_cb_user_data = user_data;
}

bool bsp_extra_player_is_playing_by_path(const char *file_path)
{
    return (strcmp(audio_file_path, file_path) == 0);
}

bool bsp_extra_player_is_playing_by_index(file_iterator_instance_t *instance, int index)
{
    return (index == file_iterator_get_index(instance));
}

#include "ui_common.h"

// Aqui a variÃ¡vel Ã© criada na memÃ³ria (sem o extern)
int32_t touch_start_x = 0;

// Aqui a funÃ§Ã£o Ã© implementada
void screen_pressed_cb(lv_event_t * e) {
    lv_indev_t * indev = lv_indev_active();
    if(indev) {
        lv_point_t p;
        lv_indev_get_point(indev, &p);
        touch_start_x = p.x; 
    }
}

void style_list_item(lv_obj_t * item) {
    if (item == NULL) return;
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(item, lv_color_black(), 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_opa(item, LV_OPA_COVER, 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(item, lv_color_white(), 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_border_width(item, 0, 0); 
    lv_obj_set_style_text_font(item, &lv_font_montserrat_20, 0); 
    lv_obj_set_style_pad_ver(item, 18, 0);  
    lv_obj_set_style_pad_column(item, 12, 0); 
}
